Trade when ahead in material; avoid trades when behind.\n6. In endgames activate king, push passed pawns, rooks to open files.\n7. }
```

> 4. Never leave a piece undefended; count attackers vs defenders before captures.
> 5. Trade when ahead in material; avoid trades when behind.
